### Executive Summary

At baseline commit `7c85a8e516165ddadb101236a88eec937bf1f095` (`main`, `2026-11-22`), all **`26 / 26` audit sections are `VERIFIED`** (`0` `QUEUED`, `0` `IN_REVIEW`, `0` `VERIFYING`), achieving 100% file/seam/test reconciliation: `496` code/config/packaging/CI/dot files (`390,128` LOC across `SEC-01`–`SEC-23`, plus `3` scoped `Cargo.toml` manifests noted in `SEC-07`/`20`/`22`), `39` cross-crate seams (`26` untrusted-input trust seams in `SEC-X1`, `13` scientific data-fidelity seams in `SEC-X2`), and `308` `.rs` integration test files + `9` `.py`/`.R` oracle reference scripts (`317` files across `22` workspace packages) + `15` `cargo xtask` policy checks (`SEC-X3`).

Independent verification resolved all `338` raw findings into **`334` unique confirmed findings** (`0` `PLAUSIBLE`; **`24` `S1`**, **`150` `S2`**, **`126` `S3`**, **`34` `S4`**) alongside **`2` `REFUTED`** (`FA-SEC21-08`, `FA-X1-04`) and **`2` `DUPLICATE`** (`FA-SEC14-06`, `FA-SEC16-01` of `FA-SEC19-02`) dispositions recorded in Section 5.

- **Release Posture (`BLOCKED` pending Wave 1):** Core mathematical foundations (`SEC-01` has `0` `S1`), dependency layer hygiene (`14/15` `cargo xtask` checks pass on tracked `main`), and prior closed remediations in `transform`, `fit`, `analysis`, and browser single-sheet clipboard/export paths hold without regressions. However, **`24` `S1` blockers** across untrusted filesystem/lock bypasses (`FA-X1-01`, `FA-SEC13-01`, `FA-SEC20-02`), archive/recovery/WASM/TeX OOM or hang hazards (`FA-SEC04-01..03`, `FA-SEC09-01`, `FA-SEC20-03`), statistical/chart wrong-results (`FA-SEC05-01..04`, `FA-SEC09-02..05`), live-link un-normalization (`FA-X2-01`), and UI modal/host/workspace data loss (`FA-SEC18-01..04`, `FA-SEC19-02..03`, `FA-SEC20-01`) require remediation before release.

## 4. Master Confirmed & Plausible Findings Table

Updated as sections transition to `VERIFIED`, sorted by settled severity (`S1` -> `S4`), then by finding ID.

| Severity | Finding ID | Section | Verdict | Category | Location | Summary |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `S1` | `FA-SEC04-01` | `SEC-04` | `CONFIRMED` | `resources` | `crates/format/src/prism.rs:247-257` | Embedded `.prism` `document.json` clones image assets (`bytes.to_vec()`) unboundedly before `validate_project_document` checks `MAX_PROJECT_IMAGE_ASSETS` and duplicate `asset_id`s. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC04-02` | `SEC-04` | `CONFIRMED` | `security` | `crates/format/src/recovery.rs:1121-1141, 1159-1165` | Oversized `curvebook.lock.evict` triggers unbounded `std::fs::read` (OOM) and restores itself via `hard_link` instead of evicting, wedging lock recovery. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC04-03` | `SEC-04` | `CONFIRMED` | `security` | `crates/format/src/recovery.rs:970-988, 1354-1360, 1423-1429, 1451-1466` | `DirectoryJournalBackend` reads snapshots, journals, and lockfiles without regular-file/symlink checks (FIFO hang) or snapshot byte caps (OOM). |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC05-01` | `SEC-05` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` | `crates/stats/src/categorical.rs:2608-2615, 3053-3150` | Contingency `PartsOfWhole` runner silently executes goodness-of-fit on kappa, Fisher, Barnard, trend, and McNemar requests (surviving `R2-S07-07`). |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC05-02` | `SEC-05` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` | `crates/stats/src/categorical.rs:2620-2631, 2905-2926` | Contingency & exact binomial extractors use `resolve_selected_indices` and `.first()`, silently dropping invalid indices, duplicating `[0, 0]`, and ignoring extra `Column` selections (`REGRESSION` / incomplete `#32`). |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC05-03` | `SEC-05` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` | `crates/stats/src/two_group.rs:1757-1778, 1826-1840, 2176-2187, 2263-2273, 2564-2575` | Two-group & MV entry points silently truncate multi-column selections, misroute `>= 2` categorical factors into numeric extraction, and expose numeric `Label` IDs on explicit selections. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC05-04` | `SEC-05` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` | `crates/stats/src/categorical.rs:774-791, 869, 1441` | Boschloo, CSM, and `noncentral_hypergeom_p_and_mean_log_or` use additive `1e-12` tolerances that inflate extreme-tail *P* values by orders of magnitude. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC09-01` | `SEC-09` | `CONFIRMED` | `no-panic` / `resources` | `crates/chart/src/equation_box.rs:681-703, 758-763, 1040-1044, 1061-1070` | Infinite parser loop & OOM abort on malformed TeX script syntax (`x^`, `x_ `, `y = ^`, `y = ^{unclosed`). |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC09-02` | `SEC-09` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` | `crates/chart/src/multivariate_layout.rs:612, 655-679, 744, 764-770, 779-797, 988-991, 1004-1022` | `CategoricalSummary` plots hardcoded `1.96` 95% CIs (ignoring Student-t), silently falls back to arithmetic mean for geometric mean, ignores error-bar domain extents, and drops point overrides/hit-test state. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC09-03` | `SEC-09` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` | `crates/chart/src/scales.rs:189-198, 1185-1191, 1246-1253, 1303-1365` | Log/explicit/extreme axis domain resolution produces inverted domains (`hi < 0.1`), drops sub-picomolar log domains (`<= 1e-12` linear span), and breaks mapping on `(-1e308, 1e308)`. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC09-04` | `SEC-09` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` / `contract-drift` | `crates/chart/src/multivariate_layout.rs:2517-2519, 2596-2627, 2635-2639` | Bland-Altman extraction ignores `xy_compare_x_vs_y` (pairing `X` vs `Y0` instead of `Y0` vs `Y1`), overrides `MultipleVariables` columns with `multivariate` spec, and hardcodes `±1.96 SD` LoA captions. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC09-05` | `SEC-09` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` | `crates/chart/src/multivariate_layout.rs:1147-1164, 1742-1750, 3349-3359, 3675` | Pre-summarized `Grouped`/`Column` subcolumns (`Mean`, `SD`, `SEM`, `N`, `IQR`) are averaged as raw replicates in Heatmaps, Normal Q-Q, and Residual plots. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC13-01` | `SEC-13` | `CONFIRMED` | `security` / `contract-drift` | `crates/ui-egui/src/{control_io.rs:2091-2101,3681-3684, gui_actions.rs:118-135, lib.rs:1434-1452}` | `control_io::write_host_file` bypasses `ExternalPathPolicy` (`Workdir` / `RejectExternal`) on `ui.screenshot` and `render.pdf` when `--gui-only` is false. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC18-01` | `SEC-18` | `CONFIRMED` | `data-loss` / `contract-drift` | `crates/ui-egui/src/egui_shell/app_modals.rs:1129-1138` | `ExportFigure` modal silently drops PDF, SVG, PNG, and TIFF exports without opening Save File or writing to disk (incomplete fix of `R15-GUI-23`; spans into `SEC-19`). |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC18-02` | `SEC-18` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` / `contract-drift` / `data-loss` | `crates/ui-egui/src/egui_shell/import_wizard.rs:433-451, 593-605, 681-714, 765-788, 1111-1138, 1152-1237` | 5-stage `Import & Paste Special` wizard shows transformed preview while silently dropping Stage 1/3/4/5 drafts on commit; `Save Preset` is unwired and `Next`/`Back` do not sync `wiz.stage`. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC18-03` | `SEC-18` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` / `data-loss` | `crates/ui-egui/src/egui_shell/graph_modals/mod.rs:337-422`, `state.rs:228-236, 288-296`, `format_graph/heatmap.rs:26-97, 205` | `Format Graph` Heatmap modal never loads existing heatmap settings and unconditionally overwrites `multivariate.color` and `x_axis.tick_angle_deg` on commit. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC18-04` | `SEC-18` | `CONFIRMED` | `wrong-result` / `contract-drift` | `crates/ui-egui/src/egui_shell/transform_modals.rs:258-260, 1463-1513, 1734-1756, 1768-1771` | Standalone `Simulate XY Curve` / `Random Sample Rows` show `Extract & Rearrange` UI and hardcode ignored parameters; `Extract & Rearrange` column bindings are dead. |

| `S1` | `FA-SEC19-03` | `SEC-19` | `CONFIRMED` | `data-loss` / `robustness` | `apps/curvebook/src/{gui_window.rs:776-838, desktop_host.rs:2253-2256,2796-2813,2902-2917,3504-3505, main.rs:472-474,504-510}` | Single-viewport `CurveBookNativeApp` hides earlier unsaved/CLI windows unreachably when a second window opens and aliases `app.quit`/`file.quit` to single-window close. |
| `S1` | `FA-SEC20-01` | `SEC-20` | `CONFIRMED` | `data-loss` / `security` | `apps/curvebook-web/{src/lib.rs:268-274,531-539, src/host_adapters.rs:977-984,1092-1105,2263-2265,2502-2525, src/main.rs:76, index.html:259-260,886-933,1440-1445,1784}` | Stale `virtual_workspace` / `activeDirectoryHandle` overwrites the previous `.curvebook.d` folder after opening another project, and hardcoded `doc_id = "default"` collides multi-tab locks and autosaves. |

## 6. Cross-Cutting Themes & Notes

- Ledger initialized at baseline `7c85a8e516165ddadb101236a88eec937bf1f095` (`main`, `2026-11-22`).
- **`SEC-01` (`VERIFIED`, 24/24 `CONFIRMED`, dynamic probe `8 passed` across `math`/`palette`/`platform`):** Zero `S1` crashes/regressions (`#64`, `#65`, `#82` intact); primary issues are extreme-scale/extreme-tail numerical accuracy (`FA-SEC01-01..04`, `FA-SEC01-07..15`, `FA-SEC01-24`) and `sync` timeout clamping (`FA-SEC01-05`).
- **`SEC-02` (`VERIFIED`, 14/14 `CONFIRMED`, dynamic probe `5 passed` in `ver_sec02_probe`):** Parameter shorthand drift (`FA-SEC02-01`) inverts `threshold`/`alpha` and misroutes `response_column` for GLM/MI/`pvalue_stack`; row shifts on `TableArchetype` desync `DataSheet::exclusion_notes`/`linked_regions` (`FA-SEC02-02`), `upstream_dependencies` strips self-cycles (`FA-SEC02-03`), `ResultSheet::lookup_scalar` exposes `Dirty` scalars (`FA-SEC02-08`, spanning into `SEC-03`), and L1 `Deserialize`/constructors bypass `SheetId`, `PowerAnalysisSpec`, `LayoutSpec`, `GraphSpec`, `MAX_PROJECT_CELLS`, and `ProjectImageAsset` validation (`FA-SEC02-04`, `FA-SEC02-06`, `FA-SEC02-07`, `FA-SEC02-11`, `FA-SEC02-12`).
- **`SEC-03` (`VERIFIED`, 16/16 `CONFIRMED`, baseline `133 passed` + `ver_sec03_probe` `16 passed`):** Closed transform remediations (`#14`, `#54`–`#63`, `#125`, `#126`) hold with zero regressions, while new findings show projection/transpose exclusion-note loss (`FA-SEC03-01..02`), unvalidated in-place `Contingency` mutations (`FA-SEC03-04`), `O(N^2)` non-ASCII `.cbtemplate` UTF-8 scanning (`FA-SEC03-08`), tidy/import/conversion edge gaps (`FA-SEC03-09..11`), and `countif(..., "true")` inversion (`FA-SEC03-13`, adjusted `S4 -> S3`; `FA-SEC03-03` adjusted `S2 -> S3` aligned with `FA-SEC02-08`).
- **`SEC-04` (`VERIFIED`, 14/14 `CONFIRMED`, dynamic probe `11 passed`):** Security/OOM gaps in `recovery.rs` (`DirectoryJournalBackend` missing `is_file()` checks before open and `reclaim_stale_eviction_lock` unbounded read + re-link loop) and `.prism` image-asset cloning before validation (`FA-SEC04-01..03`), plus two regressions (`FA-SEC04-04` `FormatRegistry` bypassing `#71` failed-result export guard, `FA-SEC04-09` excluded text CSV round-trip `#73`) and `apply_csv_import_link` partial/non-rectangular mutation defects (`FA-SEC04-06..08`). Also `v0..=v6` migration DOM budget (`FA-SEC04-05`) blocks projects above ~250k cells.
- **`SEC-05` (`VERIFIED`, 13/13 `CONFIRMED`, dynamic probe `7 passed`):** Column-selection truncation/silent drops across Contingency/Binomial/Two-Group/MV (`FA-SEC05-02` partial regression vs `#32`, `FA-SEC05-03`), surviving `R2-S07-07` `PartsOfWhole` method bypass (`FA-SEC05-01`), additive `1e-12` Boschloo/CSM tail inflation (`FA-SEC05-04`), and solver/contract mismatches (`FA-SEC05-05..11`).
- **`SEC-06` (`VERIFIED`, 11/11 `CONFIRMED`, baseline `292 passed` + `ver_sec06_probe` `11 passed`):** Pre-summarized guards and multi-replicate support remain uneven (`RepeatedMeasures`/`Friedman` accepting pre-summarized `ColumnTable`s, MV & Passing–Bablok reading only subcolumn `0`), public ANOVA/REML entrypoints panic on malformed observation/term inputs (`FA-SEC06-03..04`), and all `46` `p_cell` sites hardcode `0.05` stars (`FA-SEC06-09`, settled `S4 -> S3`, completing the `FA-SEC05-12` pattern across `crates/stats`).
- **`SEC-07` (`VERIFIED`, 16/16 `CONFIRMED`, baseline `228 passed` + `ver_sec07_probe` `15 passed`):** Prior solver remediations (`#10`–`#12`, `#80`, `#114`–`#116`, `#121`) hold (`0` regressions), while new findings reveal silent masking of diverged/IRLS-capped/unconverged fits (`FA-SEC07-01..02`, `FA-SEC07-06`), pooled-vs-per-dataset constant reporting drift (`FA-SEC07-03`), Cauchy weighted-SS misattribution (`FA-SEC07-04`), weighted pre-summarized `N`/`SD` loss (`FA-SEC07-05`), MV index collisions (`FA-SEC07-07`), Deming pre-summarized & `n = 3` duplicate failures (`FA-SEC07-08`), and one-sided boundary gradient/TeX fallback gaps (`FA-SEC07-09..15`; `FA-SEC07-09` adjusted `S2 -> S3`).
- **`SEC-08` (`VERIFIED`, 12/12 `CONFIRMED`, baseline `228 passed` + `ver_sec08_probe` `11 passed`):** Closed DSL/library fixes hold (`0` regressions), while new findings expose `<A>`/`<~A>` main-equation branch shadowing by trailing defaults (`FA-SEC08-01`), `MorrisonKi` cancellation returning uninhibited `Vo` (`FA-SEC08-02`), `OneSiteBinding` (`with_hill`) `NaN` (`FA-SEC08-03`) and `RichardsFivePl` `LogEC50 = ±Inf` inside `default_bounds()` (`FA-SEC08-04`), plus lowercase local `t` fallback to predictor `X` (`FA-SEC08-05`) and multi-branch ODE missing-IC validation gaps (`FA-SEC08-08`).
- **`SEC-09` (`VERIFIED`, 13/13 `CONFIRMED`):** Chart compilation exhibits three parallel axis/chrome/annotation implementations (`scales.rs`, `xy.rs`, `survival_cat.rs`) causing feature drift, silent fallbacks across `CategoricalSummary` / `BlandAltman` / pre-summarized tables / horizontal charts, and an infinite loop/OOM hazard in the TeX script parser (`FA-SEC09-01`).
- **`SEC-10` (`VERIFIED`, 11/11 `CONFIRMED`):** Zero-bundled-font builds (`BUNDLED_FONTS = &[]`) fall back to placeholder boxes (`□`) for CJK/RTL/extended Unicode in outlined raster/SVG/PDF paths instead of querying `SystemFontSet` (`FA-SEC10-04`), PDF `/F6` embedding lacks `cmap` coverage & style support (`FA-SEC10-01..02`), and `from_scene_capped` retains `#9`-style independent axis clamping on MCP/control raster exports (`FA-SEC10-03`).
- **`SEC-11` (`VERIFIED`, 10/10 `CONFIRMED`):** `methods_report` mislabels `Xy` Bland-Altman `X`-vs-`Y[0]` titles (`FA-SEC11-01`) and formats `HookSource` via Rust `Debug` (`FA-SEC11-07`); `cmd::helpers::decode_base64` lacks payload size caps (`FA-SEC11-02`); `sheet.extractResultTab` skips `MAX_PROJECT_CELLS` (`FA-SEC11-03`); `project.open`/`save`/`createCheckpoint` drift on `dirty` state and WAL checkpointing/timestamps (`FA-SEC11-04..05`); `clone_downstream_graphs` misses annotation/inline hooks (`FA-SEC11-06`); `10` `sheet.*` schemas misadvertise `"additionalProperties": true` (`FA-SEC11-08`); and `cmd::helpers::write_output_file` skips `ExternalPathPolicy`/atomic writes (`FA-SEC11-09`).
- **`SEC-12` (`VERIFIED`, 11/11 `CONFIRMED`, baseline `269 passed` + `ver_sec12_probe` `7 passed`):** Closed `analysis.rs` fixes hold (`0` regressions), while `table.convertArchetype` drops 1-Y `Y0` / re-targets 2-Y `Y1` `point_overrides` onto `Y0` (via `recalculate_dirty_with_links` -> `prune_out_of_bounds_point_overrides`) and leaves stale `spec.multivariate` (`FA-SEC12-01`), `7` table import/simulation commands bypass `MAX_PROJECT_CELLS` (`FA-SEC12-02`), inline `bytes`/`csv` bypass archive/CSV caps (`FA-SEC12-03`), `setCalculatedVariable` overwrites `linked_regions` (`FA-SEC12-04`), `graph.setAxes`/`setSpec`/`layout.setSpec` & typed-object `transform.run` `Simulate` silently drop missing/shorthand/non-object fields (`FA-SEC12-05..06`, `FA-SEC12-10`), `28` `table.*` schemas advertise `"additionalProperties": true` (`19` reject vs `9` `#[serde(flatten)]` silently drop, `FA-SEC12-07`), and `pasteLink`/`into_cell` drop invalid `link_spec`/`decimal_separator` errors (`FA-SEC12-08..09`).
- **`SEC-13` (`VERIFIED`, 14/14 `CONFIRMED`):** `write_host_file` bypasses `ExternalPathPolicy` on `ui.screenshot`/`render.pdf` (`FA-SEC13-01`); `try_dispatch_egui_backend` skips `catch_unwind` and per-request token checks (`FA-SEC13-03`); atomic `command.batch` rollback fails silently past undo-stack eviction (`FA-SEC13-02`); and text/paste/inspect paths exhibit shortcut & widget-coverage drift (`FA-SEC13-06..14`).
- **`SEC-14` (`VERIFIED`, 12/13 unique `CONFIRMED`; `FA-SEC14-06` settled `DUPLICATE` of `FA-SEC19-02`):** Non-modal canvas dialogs and grid selection suffer from round-trip state drops (`PairwiseComparisonsState` clearing/multi-bracket handling, `BubblePlotInspectorState` losing `x_level_order_override`, `FormatAxesState` losing edits across axis tabs, `EditAnalysisParametersState` dropping PCR outcome and Contingency/Two-Group fields, `InsertModelEquation` duplicating over existing equations, `FA-SEC14-01..05`, `FA-SEC14-13`), plus `GridViewState` discontiguous/empty selection and `decimal_sep` hoist mismatches (`FA-SEC14-07`, `FA-SEC14-09..10`).
- **`SEC-15` (`VERIFIED`, 15/15 `CONFIRMED`):** View-shard dispatchers and localization reveal unbounded keyboard range materialization in `grid_core` (`FA-SEC15-01`), discontiguous-range collapse + full-sheet unlink wiping + full-doc `.clone()` in `toolbar_menus`/`grid_core` (`FA-SEC15-02`, `FA-SEC15-10`), `Import & Paste Special` header/row-title/external-path bugs (`FA-SEC15-03..04`), `mv.metric:Count` misrouting to `GeometricMean` (`FA-SEC15-05`), wrong-tab `results::handle_copy_action` lookup (`FA-SEC15-13`), plus untranslated i18n terms and leaked `"QOL-7"` tags (`FA-SEC15-14`).
- **`SEC-16` (`VERIFIED`, 11/12 unique `CONFIRMED`; `FA-SEC16-01` settled `DUPLICATE` of `FA-SEC19-02`):** Chrome/shell wiring exhibits `R15-GUI-27` regression on Tier-2 `+ Constant` overwriting `"Constant"` (`FA-SEC16-02`), raw `egui::Key` global/dock shortcuts bypassing `ShortcutRegistry` (`FA-SEC16-03`), Navigator cascade-delete modal bypassing `HybridCoexistenceArbiter` and `ui.inspect` (`FA-SEC16-04`), hardcoded `rtl_mirrored: false` alongside missing curated Arabic/Hebrew font queries (`FA-SEC16-05..06`), and Find Next / footer popover / split-pane `Insert Image…` state drift (`FA-SEC16-08..10`).
- **`SEC-17` (`VERIFIED`, 11/11 `CONFIRMED`, 23/23 files PASS):** Trailing Studio reads moved-out `app.egui_views.canvas_view` (`None` on desktop, `FA-SEC17-01`) and leaves `layout_margin_mm`/`snap_enabled`/`table_decimals` unwired (`FA-SEC17-03`), `info_view` drops unsaved dirty notes on sheet/hook navigation (`FA-SEC17-02`), multi-archetype `data_grid` misses frozen/linked-region guards and popover/`will_discard` parity (`FA-SEC17-04`, `FA-SEC17-09`), and `results_view`/`welcome_hub`/`drill_back`/`trailing_studio` exhibit split-view copy, `Insert Image`, shortcut, and RTL-mirroring drift (`FA-SEC17-05..08`, `FA-SEC17-10`).
- **`SEC-18` (`VERIFIED`, 20/20 `CONFIRMED`):** Modal dialogs exhibit a recurring UI-vs-engine state split where `egui_shell` local drafts are unsynced on open (`heatmap_*`, `PowerStudioEguiState`), discarded on commit (`ImportWizardEguiState` stage 1/3/4/5 options, `extract_binding_*`, `title_draft`/`rename_draft`, `diag_*`), or mutated immediately past `Cancel` (`Preferences`, `Manage Variables`). Desktop host routing (`DESKTOP_HOST_ROUTED_COMMANDS`) also covers `EMF` export while dropping PDF/SVG/PNG/TIFF (`FA-SEC18-01`, spanning into `SEC-19`).
- **`SEC-19` (`VERIFIED`, 11/11 `CONFIRMED`):** Desktop host export routing (`FA-SEC19-02` completing the `FA-SEC18-01` seam) drops native Save File pickers and non-path options across in-window/dialog commands; single-viewport session switching hides unsaved/CLI windows and blocks multi-window `app.quit` (`FA-SEC19-03`), while tool/control seams suffer from `graph.addComparisonBrackets` / `table.paste` schema drift (`FA-SEC19-01`, `FA-SEC19-06`), missing shortcut routes (`FA-SEC19-04`), and `ui.pointer_gesture` fallback/ordering bugs (`FA-SEC19-05`).
- **`SEC-20` (`VERIFIED`, 10/10 `CONFIRMED`):** Browser host fixes from `#123` keep single-sheet export/clipboard and pinch-cancel intact (`#123.4`, `#123.6`), while residual gaps allow stale `virtual_workspace` / `activeDirectoryHandle` overwrites and hardcoded `"default"` lock/autosave collisions (`FA-SEC20-01`), read-only lock bypass via `UiCommand` canvas presets and preference persistence (`FA-SEC20-02`), uncapped `16384px` WASM RGBA OOM allocations (`FA-SEC20-03`), and modal/a11y/key/localStorage drift between Rust `chrome_bridge` and `index.html` (`FA-SEC20-04..08`).
- **`SEC-21` (`VERIFIED`, 7/8 `CONFIRMED`, 1 `REFUTED` [`FA-SEC21-08`]):** Live `--control-port` and GUI MCP tools exhibit schema-vs-runtime drift (`def_ui_pointer_gesture` `metrics` field names in `FA-SEC21-01`, `ui_inspect` vs `docs/mcp.md` in `FA-SEC21-02`, unenforced `additionalProperties: false` in `FA-SEC21-05`), `16 MiB` control-line / `5s` socket-timeout exhaustion (`FA-SEC21-03..04`), and missing symlink/ownership checks on explicit `CURVEBOOK_CONFIG_DIR`/`control-*.token` (`FA-SEC21-07`); `FA-SEC21-08` was refuted because `<= MAX_HEADER_COUNT` correctly allows 32 headers plus the `\r\n` terminator.
- **`SEC-22` (`VERIFIED`, 19/19 `CONFIRMED`, `15/15` src files + `xtask/Cargo.toml` PASS):** Cargo 1.77+ `@`-less `package_id` parsing breaks CI cache retention for `xtask`, `curvebook`, and `curvebook-cli` (`FA-SEC22-01`), while policy gates share structural scanner bypasses: `brands` decoding UTF-8 before BOM-less UTF-16 (`FA-SEC22-02`) and skipping escapes/SVG entities/text extensions/tracked ZIPs (`FA-SEC22-07..08`), `telemetry` line-splitting TOML/workflows and missing `.github/actions/**` and scheme-source CSP rules (`FA-SEC22-03..06`), `secrets` skipping NUL/UTF-16 and `++ ` diff lines (`FA-SEC22-09`), `layers` allowing namespace aliases (`FA-SEC22-11`), `cleanroom`/`brands`/`assets` failing open on depth `>= 2` or malformed `dist/` ZIPs (`FA-SEC22-12`), and a bare `"|"` markdown slice panic (`FA-SEC22-13`).
- **`SEC-23` (`VERIFIED`, 16/16 `CONFIRMED`, 96/96 files PASS):** Release verification and operational gates expose untrusted `.AppImage` fallback execution + unflagged tar/rpm archive listing (`FA-SEC23-01`), two-column corpus manifest traversal (`FA-SEC23-02`), missing `clippy::indexing_slicing = "deny"` enforcement (`FA-SEC23-03`), `status`/`citations` AST & block-comment parser gaps (`FA-SEC23-04..05`), `docs-site` fail-open HTML/image/snapshot checks (`FA-SEC23-08`), `ATTRIBUTION.md` omission across non-MSIX packagers (`FA-SEC23-06`), unverified preinstalled `appimagetool` / missing `import-cert.sh` cleanup trap (`FA-SEC23-07`), and non-atomic `fs::write` / unbounded `Command` in 11 `xtask` commands (`FA-SEC23-14`).
- **`SEC-X1` (`VERIFIED`, 5/6 `CONFIRMED`, 1 `REFUTED` [`FA-X1-04`], 26/26 seams across Families 1–4 `PASS`):** End-to-end untrusted-input boundary tracing uncovered `--gui-only` filesystem read bypass via `ui_invoke_menu` `Caller::Ui` escalation when `--workdir` is absent (`FA-X1-01`, `S1`), missing pre-read byte/entry caps and `manifest.json` verification on browser -> WASM -> `VirtualWorkspaceFolder` ingestion (`FA-X1-02`; precision notes: `open_project_json_bytes` calls `validate_project_document` at `lib.rs:259` while skipping cumulative `MAX_PROJECT_CELLS`, and `preferences_set` validates post-parse while `open_file_bytes`/`import_dropped_files`/`import_directory_entries` omit caps), unbudgeted graph annotation RGBA amplification (`FA-X1-03`; overlaps `FA-SEC09-12` `S3`, escalated to `S2` across `doc`/`chart`/`layout`/`pdf`), independent CLI raster edge clamping (`FA-X1-05`, also `cmd_bench_perf::rasterize_and_encode_png`), and desktop `FileDropped` policy/adapter loss (`FA-X1-06`). `FA-X1-04` was refuted because Rust `DirEntry::metadata` does not follow symlinks (`direntry_follows_symlink = false`, `loop_hung = false`; Seam #4 precision note: CLI module `cmd_bench_perf.rs` exposes `bench`/`perf`, not `bench-perf`).
- **`SEC-X2` (`VERIFIED`, 8/8 `CONFIRMED`, 13/13 seams across Families 1–4 `PASS`):** End-to-end scientific data-fidelity tracing confirms zero unreferenced overlap with `SEC-01`–`SEC-23` while uncovering `S1` silent un-normalization and un-exclusion on live DAG refreshes (`ImportLinkSpec` omits `baseline`/`exclude_row_predicate`, `FA-X2-01`) alongside `S2`/`S3` cross-crate contract splits: `AnalyzableResultSheet` (`tab_id: None`) source flips on refresh (`FA-X2-02`), unknown-sample validation aborts on omitted `SD`/`N` (`FA-X2-03`), `X` replicate/summary subcolumns collapsed to `X[0]` outside `chart` (`FA-X2-04`; `two_group.rs:2512` row-label only), pre-summarized `ColumnTable` pooling/`%CV`/`n=1` divergence (`FA-X2-05`; `summarize_pre_summarized_points` at `column.rs:461-498`), multi-sheet `PointStyleOverride.series_index` collisions & missing `InsertRows`/paste/refresh/`run_exclude_cells` pruning (`FA-X2-06`; `table.rs:1893`), `GeneratedSequence` X/exclusion drops in CSV and `sheets/*.csv` archive sidecars (`FA-X2-07`), and `backfill_confidence_interval_cells` ignoring `transpose`/`ImportFilterSpec`/`skip_contiguous_blanks` (`FA-X2-08`).
- **`SEC-X3` (`VERIFIED`, 10/10 `CONFIRMED`, 308 `.rs` tests + 9 ref scripts + 15 `cargo xtask` checks `PASS`):** Policy gate audit confirmed `14/15` checks exit `0` on tracked `main` while `cargo xtask secrets` fails (`EXIT=1`, `443` initial / `496` verifier-run hits) solely on untracked `docs/audit/full-2026-11-22/SEC-*.md` home-path links as `brands`/`cleanroom` skip `docs/audit/` and `ci policy` conditionally gates `commands`/`docs-site` on `full-gates` (`FA-X3-06`). Test suite forensics across `22` workspace packages revealed formula-bar `Err(Conflict)` discarded (`let _ =`) alongside `25` metadata-only `verify_egui_inspect_and_shot` tests (`FA-X3-01`), four suites omitting PCR/`ExportFigure` SVG assertions (`FA-X3-02`), silent loopback `TimedOut`/`PermissionDenied` passes and unguarded external-viewer/`macos_clipboard_live` skips (`FA-X3-03`; precision note: `gui_only_surface.rs` & `mcp_e2e.rs` assert `io_error` fail-closed before returning early), mock-schema drift + skipped post-patch readback + `ChangeGraphType` bypasses (`FA-X3-04`), an `#[ignore]`d 1M-cell release gate excluded from `PERFORMANCE_FILTER` (`FA-X3-05`), vacuous `if let Some` assertions & mislabeled `human_wf_web_parity` checks (`FA-X3-07..08`), `transform`/`fit`/`stats`/`engine` D1/dirty-flag test gaps (`FA-X3-09`), and `35` discarded `set_locale` returns masking `"en"` fallbacks (`FA-X3-10`).

---

## 7. Top Systemic Themes

1. **UI Shell / Modal Draft Split & Export File-Routing Bypass (`SEC-14`, `SEC-16`, `SEC-18`, `SEC-19`, `SEC-X3`):** Multiple egui dialogs (`ImportWizardEguiState`, `heatmap_*`, `transform_modals`, `PairwiseComparisonsState`, `FormatAxesState`, `EditAnalysisParametersState`, `PowerStudioEguiState`) maintain local UI drafts decoupled from engine commits, discarding or overwriting user settings, while `DESKTOP_HOST_ROUTED_COMMANDS` misses export/template/import commands (`FA-SEC18-01..04`, `FA-SEC19-02..03`, `FA-SEC14-01..05`). Metadata-only `verify_egui_inspect_and_shot` and dialog-closure tests (`FA-X3-01`, `FA-X3-02`, `FA-X3-04`) masked these failures in CI.
2. **Trust-Boundary Escalation, Uncapped Ingress & Memory Amplification (`SEC-04`, `SEC-11`, `SEC-13`, `SEC-20`, `SEC-21`, `SEC-X1`):** Secondary entry paths bypass core security and allocation budgets: `ui_invoke_menu` runs arbitrary `"params"` under `Caller::Ui` without `--workdir` confinement (`FA-X1-01`), `write_host_file` skips `ExternalPathPolicy` (`FA-SEC13-01`), desktop `FileDropped` strips `path_policy`/`human_path_only`/adapters (`FA-X1-06`), Web read-only locks & workspace handles collide/bypass (`FA-SEC20-01`, `FA-SEC20-02`), and Browser/WASM/`VirtualWorkspaceFolder`, `.prism` image cloning, `DirectoryJournalBackend`, `WebTextBridge`, TeX parser loops, and unbudgeted `GraphSpec.annotations` RGBA decodes enable OOM or FIFO hangs (`FA-SEC04-01..03`, `FA-SEC09-01`, `FA-SEC20-03`, `FA-X1-02`, `FA-X1-03`).
3. **Cross-Crate Scientific Fidelity & Pre-Summarized / Replicate Contract Drift (`SEC-02`, `SEC-03`, `SEC-05`, `SEC-06`, `SEC-07`, `SEC-08`, `SEC-09`, `SEC-X2`):** Subcolumn modes (`MeanSdN`, `MeanSemN`, `MeanCvN`, limits, medians, `RawReplicates`) and live DAG regions diverge across crate boundaries: `ImportLinkSpec` drops `baseline`/`exclude_row_predicate` on refresh (`FA-X2-01`), `AnalyzableResultSheet` refreshes swap sources (`FA-X2-02`), `X` replicates collapse to `X[0]` outside `chart` (`FA-X2-04`), parametric unknown-sample interpolation aborts without `SD`/`N` (`FA-X2-03`), One-Way ANOVA & column charts drop multi-row pooling (`FA-X2-05`), `PointStyleOverride` collides/desyncs across pastes and sheets (`FA-X2-06`, `FA-SEC12-01`), `GeneratedSequence` drops X/exclusions in CSV & archive sidecars (`FA-X2-07`), statistical runners truncate selections/method routing (`FA-SEC05-01..03`), and chart pipelines disagree across `scales.rs`, `xy.rs`, `survival_cat.rs`, and `multivariate_layout.rs` (`FA-SEC09-02..05`).
4. **Secondary-Path Invariant Bypasses across Serialization, Schemas & Bulk Shorthands (`SEC-02`, `SEC-11`, `SEC-12`, `SEC-21`):** Deserializers, `command`/`sheet`/`table` shorthands, and inline byte/CSV inputs bypass `MAX_PROJECT_CELLS` (`50M`), `decode_base64` caps, self-cycle detection (`upstream_dependencies`), row-shift alignment (`exclusion_notes`, `linked_regions`), and strict JSON schema validation (`additionalProperties` drift between spec and runtime).
5. **Solver Convergence Masking & Numerical Extreme-Range Breakdown (`SEC-01`, `SEC-05`, `SEC-06`, `SEC-07`, `SEC-08`):** Unflagged diverged/IRLS-capped fits (`FA-SEC07-01..02`, `FA-SEC07-06`), unweighted/pre-summarized weight collapse (`FA-SEC07-05`), DSL branch shadowing & invalid bounds (`FA-SEC08-01..04`), noncentral/PTRS/Pearson cancellation (`FA-SEC01-01..04`), and discrete stats additive tail inflation (`FA-SEC05-04`) affect edge scientific accuracy.
6. **Policy Scanner Bypass & Non-Fail-Closed Test Infrastructure (`SEC-22`, `SEC-23`, `SEC-X3`):** `xtask` scanners exhibit encoding, symlink, ZIP-depth, and regex/line-parser gaps (`FA-SEC22-01..13`, `FA-SEC23-03..08`), packagers omit `ATTRIBUTION.md` or SHA-256 verification (`FA-SEC23-01`, `FA-SEC23-06..07`), while integration suites retain silent `TimedOut`/`PermissionDenied`/tool skips (`FA-X3-03`), discarded fallible/locale checks (`FA-X3-01`, `FA-X3-07`, `FA-X3-10`), `#[ignore]`d release gates (`FA-X3-05`), and working-tree `xtask secrets` failures on audit markdown local paths (`FA-X3-06`).

---

## 8. Prioritized Remediation Waves

### Wave 1 — Immediate Release Blockers (`24` `S1` Settled Findings)

Every `S1` finding is release-blocking across security isolation, runtime stability, scientific correctness, and user data integrity:

- **1A. Security Isolation, Crash/Hang & DoS Prevention (`8` findings):**
  - `FA-X1-01` (`ui_invoke_menu` `--gui-only` `Caller::Ui` escalation & embedded `ExternalDelimitedFile` read bypass)
  - `FA-SEC13-01` (`control_io::write_host_file` bypassing `ExternalPathPolicy` on `ui.screenshot` / `render.pdf`)
  - `FA-SEC20-02` (Web contended read-only lock bypass via `UiCommand`, preference writes, and `is_command_read_only`)
  - `FA-SEC04-01` (`.prism` embedded `document.json` unbounded image cloning before validation)
  - `FA-SEC04-02` (`curvebook.lock.evict` unbounded `fs::read` OOM & `hard_link` self-restore)
  - `FA-SEC04-03` (`DirectoryJournalBackend` missing regular-file/symlink checks & snapshot byte caps)
  - `FA-SEC20-03` (uncapped `WebTextBridge` RGBA/backdrop allocations causing `wasm32` OOM & integer truncation)
  - `FA-SEC09-01` (`equation_box.rs` infinite TeX script parser loop & OOM)
- **1B. Scientific Results, Charting & Live-Link Fidelity (`9` findings):**
  - `FA-SEC05-01` (`PartsOfWhole` contingency runner executing goodness-of-fit across other methods)
  - `FA-SEC05-02` (Contingency & binomial `resolve_selected_indices`/`.first()` silent index drop/duplication)
  - `FA-SEC05-03` (Two-group & MV silent column truncation, categorical misrouting, and raw `Label` leakage)
  - `FA-SEC05-04` (Boschloo/CSM/`noncentral_hypergeom` additive `1e-12` extreme-tail *P*-value inflation)
  - `FA-SEC09-02` (`CategoricalSummary` hardcoded `1.96` CI, geometric mean fallback, and error-bar/hit-test drops)
  - `FA-SEC09-03` (log/explicit/extreme axis domain inversions & sub-picomolar `<= 1e-12` log drops)
  - `FA-SEC09-04` (Bland-Altman `X`-vs-`Y0` pairing bug, MV spec override, and hardcoded `±1.96 SD` captions)
  - `FA-SEC09-05` (pre-summarized subcolumns averaged as raw replicates in Heatmaps, Normal Q-Q, & Residual plots)
  - `FA-X2-01` (`ImportLinkSpec` dropping `baseline` & `exclude_row_predicate` on `dag::refresh_single_region`)
- **1C. UI Modal, Desktop Host & Web Workspace Data Loss (`7` findings):**
  - `FA-SEC18-01` & `FA-SEC19-02` (`ExportFigure` / in-window export / save template Save File bypass & silent drop)
  - `FA-SEC18-02` (`Import & Paste Special` 5-stage wizard discarding Stage 1/3/4/5 drafts & unwired `Save Preset`)
  - `FA-SEC18-03` (`Format Graph` Heatmap modal failing to load state and overwriting color & tick angles)
  - `FA-SEC18-04` (`Simulate XY Curve` / `Random Sample Rows` / `Extract & Rearrange` broken UI/bindings)
  - `FA-SEC19-03` (single-viewport `CurveBookNativeApp` hiding earlier windows & aliasing `app.quit`)
  - `FA-SEC20-01` (Web stale `virtual_workspace`/`activeDirectoryHandle` overwrite & `"default"` lock/autosave collision)

### Wave 2 — High-Impact Cross-Crate Contracts, Solvers, UI & Gate Integrity (`150` `S2` Settled Findings)

1. **End-to-End Scientific & Solver Contracts:** Unify `AnalyzableResultSheet` link resolution (`FA-X2-02`), unknown-sample parametric Y extraction (`FA-X2-03`), `XyTable` replicate/summary `X` evaluation (`FA-X2-04`), pre-summarized `ColumnTable` pooling across `stats` & `chart` (`FA-X2-05`), `PointStyleOverride` `GraphSpec::series` keying and live-edit shifting/pruning (`FA-X2-06`, `FA-SEC12-01`), `GeneratedSequence` CSV/sidecar export (`FA-X2-07`), non-diverging fit/IRLS convergence flags & weighted `(N, SD)` handling (`FA-SEC07-01..08`), DSL branch/bounds safety (`FA-SEC08-01..04`), `TableArchetype::shift_rows_down` (`FA-SEC02-02`), and `SEC-01` numerical tail/overflow fixes (`FA-SEC01-01..05`).
2. **Ingress Resource Budgets & Trust-Policy Hardening:** Enforce pre-read byte/entry caps and `manifest.json` verification on Browser -> WASM -> `VirtualWorkspaceFolder` (`FA-X1-02`), graph `pixel_budget` + `PdfImageRegistry` pre-allocation caps (`FA-X1-03`), `RenderTargetSpec::from_scene_dpi` in CLI raster export (`FA-X1-05`), `MAX_PROJECT_CELLS` & inline payload bounds across `doc`/`engine` (`FA-SEC02-04`, `FA-SEC11-02..03`, `FA-SEC12-02`), and `--control-port` live payload/timeout fixes (`FA-SEC21-01,03,04`).
3. **Interactive UI Modals, Navigation & Chrome Parity:** Resolve round-trip draft drops in canvas/modal inspectors (`FA-SEC14-01..05,13`, `FA-SEC18-05..13`), fix `GridViewState` discontiguous/sparse selection & `decimal_sep` hoisting (`FA-SEC14-07,09,10`, `FA-SEC15-01..07`), enforce `ShortcutRegistry` & modal focus arbitration (`FA-SEC16-02..04`), wire `trailing_studio`/`info_view` dirty persistence (`FA-SEC17-01..04`), and close desktop/web dialog & clipboard parity gaps (`FA-SEC13-02..09`, `FA-SEC19-01,04..07`, `FA-SEC20-04..08`).
4. **Release Gates, Policy Scanners & CI Verification Integrity:** Fix Cargo `package_id` cache parsing, scanner encoding/escaping/ZIP/TOML gaps, and AST/comment parsers in `xtask` (`FA-SEC22-01..15`, `FA-SEC23-03..08`), harden packaging/verification scripts (`FA-SEC23-01..02`), make loopback/viewer/workflow tests fail closed with real output assertions (`FA-X3-01..04`), re-enable the 1M-cell release benchmark in `PERFORMANCE_FILTER` (`FA-X3-05`), and remove home-path URIs from untracked audit files / scope `docs/audit/` cleanly (`FA-X3-06`).

### Wave 3 — Robustness, Schema Alignment, Edge Cases & Hygiene (`126` `S3` + `34` `S4` Settled Findings)

1. **`S3` Robustness, Contract Alignment & Edge Coverage (`126` findings):** Fix desktop `FileDropped` policy/adapter preservation (`FA-X1-06`), `backfill_confidence_interval_cells` filter/transpose alignment (`FA-X2-08`), `cmd`/`mcp`/`format` JSON schema strictness, `--workdir` parent creation, token permission checks, `is_dirty()` tracking, and `atomic`/`ProcessCommand` adoption (`FA-SEC11-04..09`, `FA-SEC12-03..10`, `FA-SEC21-02,05..07`, `FA-SEC23-09..14`), expand D1 `±Inf`/tie/Pratt/`is_dirty()` integration coverage and true Web UI parity tests (`FA-X3-07..09`), and complete `SEC-01`–`SEC-10` numerical/axis/font/PDF edge hardening.
2. **`S4` Code Cleanliness, Documentation & Test Hygiene (`34` findings):** Remove unused `Cargo.toml` dependencies (`FA-SEC01-22`, `FA-SEC10-11`, `FA-SEC11-10`, `FA-SEC23-16`), dead i18n/modal bindings (`FA-SEC15-15`, `FA-SEC18-20`), minor parser/CLI flag edge cases (`FA-SEC19-11`, `FA-SEC22-19`, `FA-SEC23-15`), docstring/diagnostic precision drifts, and assert all `35` `set_locale` test return values (`FA-X3-10`).
